# This Week in Marketing: Everything is an ad network, but AI agents are buying everything

![rw-book-cover](https://substackcdn.com/icons/substack/apple-touch-icon-1024x1024.png)

## Metadata
- Author: [[Jaskaran from The Social Juice]]
- Full Title: This Week in Marketing: Everything is an ad network, but AI agents are buying everything
- Category: #articles
- Summary: CES 2026 showed AI agents changing how ads are bought and used across many platforms. Big companies like Meta, Google, and Amazon are adding AI tools to improve shopping and advertising. Deepfake issues and new tech laws are raising concerns about AI’s impact on content and safety.

##### What was that? CES 2026 was weird: AI agents vs. more ads. Reddit challenges Meta, Discord goes for an IPO, Pinterest on TV, a lot happened, all the while Grok continued its CSAM spree.

The first full week of 2026 was pretty full and kind of consuming.

This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/f5b89801-d7c6-48f3-a6d1-ba71166602d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.

> Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/ce3c2b5a-b7e5-457f-8a1b-8d7ceb552c09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Reddit](https://substack.com/redirect/5f68fa55-f8d6-43b3-834f-d65907ed7539?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches automated ‘[max campaigns](https://substack.com/redirect/a613d868-6594-48a6-b498-76c33cb978e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to simplify ad buying and surface deeper audience insights.
* Pinterest’s [new shoppable CTV series](https://substack.com/redirect/1ca1ecc3-3b37-400c-be26-44b43ed3a769?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) turns pins Into purchases on [Roku](https://substack.com/redirect/6e730941-c302-4b7f-8103-1d1ac7708cba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Discord](https://substack.com/redirect/fe66e4c9-ceb5-495c-bc3a-971f629ba6b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is said to file [confidentially](https://substack.com/redirect/f92150a9-8f22-4013-9af4-734267c45002?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for IPO.
* OpenAI unveils [ChatGPT Health](https://substack.com/redirect/3fee5d0f-064e-470e-b3cc-d116c0dffc77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), says 230 million users [ask about health](https://substack.com/redirect/a1b1f9be-b01f-4719-ada6-9da1f98df868?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) each week.
* No, Grok [hasn’t paywalled](https://substack.com/redirect/33c78443-a6b9-4f81-87a9-c72605a9f77f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) its deepfake image feature.
	+ Senators [urged](https://substack.com/redirect/686c60b6-97d5-459a-b610-f610a5784781?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Apple and Google to [remove X and Grok](https://substack.com/redirect/f7b036fc-08c8-404d-abd2-631d45e455e7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from app stores over sexual deepfakes.
* Google: Don’t make “[bite-sized](https://substack.com/redirect/d7e5b8dc-2b36-473f-9ecb-a0a5e9f43a4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” content for LLMs if you care about search rank.

	+ Social media ban for under-15s: What we know about the [French government’s plan](https://substack.com/redirect/04f3a0ef-c2ae-4ea9-a8c3-ca6ba5c639f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Amazon’s AI assistant [comes to the web](https://substack.com/redirect/744eb6ab-04d4-4bda-82bb-c89b853f9700?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Alexa.com.
* [Smart Rings](https://substack.com/redirect/e2cc93c2-0b4d-4a25-ba28-c2ef4e4a26ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) poised to go mainstream after a blockbuster 2025.
* [Nvidia](https://substack.com/redirect/e8d865a4-0a9f-43a3-bb3c-fb7a104ce5ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) hires Google Veteran as its first Chief Marketing Officer.

> In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/3f8b8db4-341e-4dab-a050-e02330405a03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

##### The Bimma x Tracksuit: [2025 Collab of the Year Report](https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is finally here…

For the first time, [Bimma Williams](https://substack.com/redirect/9b1a918f-45aa-4c34-b46b-9d1c2a610168?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They [analyzed](https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy.

And built the [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.

Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked and why:

* Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.
* Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists

> It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this [Collab of the Year](https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) report.

* [Microsoft](https://substack.com/redirect/40b72fc1-7475-4224-8d14-07cb2796bfaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches Copilot Checkout and Brand Agents.
	+ Google [announces](https://substack.com/redirect/2813b1b3-40fe-4bd1-8971-3ebb3285d8a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI Mode Checkout Protocol, Business Agent.
	+ Walmart [opens up ads](https://substack.com/redirect/b2f6622b-e63a-4d72-a82c-848a8a534313?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Gen AI Shopping Agent Sparky.
	+ Google, Shopify and retailers push [AI shopping](https://substack.com/redirect/b9a3cd55-bbd1-4ba0-a40b-2ae90c5fd7f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) standard.
* Amazon’s AI shopping tool [sparks backlash](https://substack.com/redirect/67a212e5-e7a4-4bc9-872f-7a70b7606ca7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from online retailers that didn’t want websites scraped.
	+ NYC Wegmans is [storing biometric data](https://substack.com/redirect/ab4e484f-7a33-461e-8933-7d2b7e1371e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on shoppers’ eyes, voices and faces.
* NBCU says [2026 Winter Olympics ads](https://substack.com/redirect/aa1fa41a-bb5a-4c7f-8b60-a0bef2c70f2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) have sold out.
	+ Disney nearly sold out of [ad inventory](https://substack.com/redirect/e5649ffe-f8c0-48b7-8baa-24fd6010a78f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for college football championship.
* [Beehiiv](https://substack.com/redirect/4d6e3548-b967-44ba-8123-177d2ee8476b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) doubles ad sales team, scaling newsletter ads business.
* Roblox [expands](https://substack.com/redirect/9d0ad9e3-bb8e-47fd-85cf-327bdf8dc68a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) advertising platform as [essential channel](https://substack.com/redirect/2dcbc55e-2393-4c9e-b685-7da80989eb81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the next generations.
* Brands spent [more than $1 billion](https://substack.com/redirect/05944813-9fd9-431a-b09a-797cca549781?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on celebrities in ads last year.
* [PayPal Ads](https://substack.com/redirect/c56b0787-aa86-4987-bada-a7cb68fdba17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches transaction graph insights and measurement [to improve](https://substack.com/redirect/cf586585-bd69-4432-bc77-de24997d28b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) commerce advertising outcomes.
* Marc Lore’s Wonder aims to [transform restaurants](https://substack.com/redirect/a377c1c5-18d6-4cea-8e94-b66f83626621?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with automation.
* Retailers like Kroger, CVS plan many [more screens for in-store ads](https://substack.com/redirect/16b36ffb-b61d-463d-aba6-3b547b823666?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2026.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dE8f!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/bbd4bdac-07c2-4b49-abee-c283963e6a54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)I usually don’t care for whatever Adam Mosseri has to say about the state of Instagram, but I guess a lot of people do, and they are writing about the [recent essay and statements](https://substack.com/redirect/14a807e0-e30f-4b56-8aa6-c1e48a2ce15c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) made by the Instagram CEO regarding the rise of AI content and the future of Instagram.

First, I sincerely think people lack context. Go through my newsletters from 2025. Meta has made a gazillion statements about AI content being the future, and then compare a decade of statements made by Zuckerberg and Mosseri. They are both on different wavelengths and always make opposite statements to create a balance. Second, half of Instagram is ads. Meta has already integrated Gen AI creative production into their advertising platform, and it’s only going to get worse.

Based on just these two points, you should be questioning yourself. Why should I trust Mosseri? Now, let’s say we trust Mosseri and the arguments still don’t hold merit.

He says, “The key risk that a platform like Instagram faces is that, as the world inevitably changes more and more quickly, the platform fails to keep up.” Most social media trends still take a couple of days to reach Instagram. What are we talking about?

Then he says, “Creators matter more. Power has been shifting from institutions to individuals for years because the internet made it so anyone with a compelling idea could find an audience.” Where is the monetization program you promised a decade ago?

He highlighted this part to make a big point. “The bar is going to shift from ‘can you create?’ to ‘can you make something that only you could create?’” Again, your company is creating fake SEO titles for Google rankings, AI-generated search tags on top of comments, and anything remotely new gets pushed aside because the algorithm cannot compare it to other content. 

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Wzd3!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F8454bae1-f724-43b2-9a6c-6c12d2b480c5_1258x431.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/023cc0de-b59d-45b9-8437-fe804f1f6bfd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)Last, he shifted the blame to camera companies, saying, “Just as AI makes polish cheap, phone cameras have made professional looking imagery ubiquitous. Both trends cheapen the aesthetic.” Dude, in 2016 to 2018, and even today, some people call out influencers using the term “Instagram face” because your platform gave rise to all types of filters and beauty touches and played a huge role in teens feeling bad about themselves. Your company tried to [bury research](https://substack.com/redirect/849e9080-eb69-4ebc-a422-3d8169ad0e3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) exposing the negative impact this content had on teenage girls.

“That feed is dead. People largely stopped sharing personal moments to feed years ago.” Again, I wonder who caused that. It happened before TikTok. You removed the chronological feed and sent teens looking for other content because your algorithm was too busy showing kids perfect influencers. And yeah, then your app also showed them soft porn. It did that again last year, while also showing drug ads.

Near the end of the essay, he says, “It will be more practical to fingerprint real media than fake media,” and it’s true. I agree with that, because it will be unprofitable to exclude free AI content that they themselves can create.

> Mosseri says, “In a world of infinite abundance and infinite doubt, the creators who can maintain trust and signal authenticity by being real, transparent, and consistent will stand out.”

> Almost a million CEOs have uttered a version of this statement on stages. 

Guess who else made authenticity and original content a big deal? It was [Google](https://substack.com/redirect/2dfdf0f0-b4d9-4008-855b-fa2f6d5f8500?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2024. And where is Google in 2026? AI overviews everywhere. 

> TL;DR: The master manipulator rebranded Instagram’s flaws as a cultural failure and used arguments you often see in every other write up about the decline of social media.

* Google Ads [introduces](https://substack.com/redirect/df495f2a-0a95-454f-8e41-d54025e43a0d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) creator search and management features for [YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/b66e0ed9-68ab-4995-870b-7e37b460f021?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🫒 Report Break: 36% percent of chief marketing officers expect to [reduce head count over the next 12 to 24 months](https://substack.com/redirect/d5001bdd-15a1-4026-97f9-fd343787c087?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) “by utilizing AI or eliminating redundancies,” according to a new survey from executive search firm Spencer Stuart based on November interviews with approximately 90 CMOs and other marketing leaders.

---

* Google launches the [Universal Commerce Protocol (UCP)](https://substack.com/redirect/5cbddef5-9951-4915-8486-8f3c90452b67?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a new open standard for agentic commerce.
* Google tests [expanded video limits](https://substack.com/redirect/b1ea92a7-a6fe-4966-843b-5f1fdda8b626?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Performance Max.
* ‘[The Core Before Christmas](https://substack.com/redirect/37380677-13d9-43c5-9f39-944ff176a680?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ – Google’s December 2025 Broad Core Update.
* Google quietly expands [asset A/B testing](https://substack.com/redirect/b5751cd3-b972-46ef-a01c-d020e502baab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to all Performance Max campaigns.
* Google’s [recommender system](https://substack.com/redirect/5aeb4283-5e91-44b0-a69b-49fe48ba2d31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) breakthrough detects semantic intent.
* Google AI Overviews are [tested and removed](https://substack.com/redirect/17782c41-3a56-4e27-bccd-d2d02e0670ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) based on engagement.
* Gemini will let you [control Google TV settings](https://substack.com/redirect/26da826d-f2c1-4e38-87a9-1b02bd61846c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), getting richer Photos support & responses.
* Google rolls out [Tag Gateway integration](https://substack.com/redirect/0bde6ca5-3846-4019-a580-2099e07873f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) via Google Cloud.
* [Google Classroom’s new tool](https://substack.com/redirect/1bcb8155-b454-4631-9820-23973abb9231?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) uses Gemini to transform lessons into podcast episodes.
* Google to allow [Prediction Markets ads](https://substack.com/redirect/64c8f5c3-a433-40bd-8ae6-9019edc7268f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) under strict rules.

> 👀 Report Break: The “[unexpected](https://substack.com/redirect/481065d3-b2f7-4e3b-adc8-f82749b300b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” growth of 3.88% saw display advertising return to being the largest revenue category in the Digital Publishers’ Revenue Index in the third quarter of 2025 after being overtaken by subscriptions in Q1 2025 (and evenly matched in Q2).

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/828152a4-e03d-42b7-8bb2-0888ce497c64?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* xAI raises [$20B](https://substack.com/redirect/6d8d2477-6dd2-4aca-8440-f9cce6eaab62?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in [Series E](https://substack.com/redirect/1bb735b3-22d1-43bc-a9c5-90f7900a4214?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), despite Grok’s ongoing CSAM spree.
	+ Why isn’t there a bigger Grok boycott? Reddit says, “[Can't boycott something I never use](https://substack.com/redirect/49e19874-95e7-4712-9d50-c003d2d93411?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).”
* X could face UK ban over [deepfakes](https://substack.com/redirect/358cb7a4-a29a-49b8-99c7-242ac45217c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), minister says.
	+ Government [accused](https://substack.com/redirect/9a31ba2b-e4aa-427e-9034-2e0460c4f383?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of dragging its heels on deepfake law over Grok AI.
* X ‘accepts its mistake’, assures it will comply with [Indian laws](https://substack.com/redirect/deda102a-9d15-401c-afe5-2d03a4084b9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), say government sources on Grok AI issue.
	+ [Malaysia](https://substack.com/redirect/bfe25fb6-c1b0-4258-b4a7-2e57a2c6b1c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and [Indonesia](https://substack.com/redirect/173db0ae-40ce-4d3d-8cfa-2f9ffda9db15?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) block access to Grok because of sexually explicit content.
* Grok is being used to [mock and strip women](https://substack.com/redirect/b9dc8bde-f5f1-4591-83a7-eceb36b83276?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in hijabs and saris.
* Elon Musk’s Grok has [friends](https://substack.com/redirect/f1fe2afa-f589-49c8-8539-c29498a3b5bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in high places.
	+ ‘Add blood, forced smile’: how Grok’s [nudification tool](https://substack.com/redirect/37d4b38f-c491-44aa-bc5a-3838f8ad8c1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) went viral.
* EU orders X to [keep Grok documents](https://substack.com/redirect/3da66295-c6d4-4820-8d42-4c2aa99fd28f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for longer amid sexualised AI photos furore.

> 🪑 Report Break: NBCUniversal sold out all inventory back in September, with prices speculated to have spiked at to $8 million for 30 seconds. It can be very difficult for brands to gain attention – we’ve observed that [more than half of Super Bowl ads achieve less than 1% brand recall](https://substack.com/redirect/be241560-5774-4352-86b4-6c865e058068?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the morning after the game.

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Nq8A!,w_56,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd6b65fcd-fe11-48ac-bfe4-6c0f746e1608_300x300.png)The Argument

[How getting richer made teenagers less free](https://substack.com/redirect/e4538313-6df9-4c1c-999e-366ebdc5fb17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

In 1913, journalist Helen Todd talked to hundreds of 14- to 16-year-olds working in American factories. Most of their fathers were dead or had crippling health issues thanks to decades of work in unsafe factories, and their mothers were supporting an average of five children on low wages. By doing pieceme…

* Stagwell turns [Sport Beach](https://substack.com/redirect/53f2816e-ce74-48ec-b974-517a3ae377ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into its own company.
* [Octave](https://substack.com/redirect/dc6e65eb-1eba-4ccb-803b-0940d060de2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to scale audience segments with Audiomob deal.
* ID5 integrates into Horizon Media’s newly launched [HorizonOS](https://substack.com/redirect/3957aa73-361b-42b8-8fb3-5fac5d53317e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Brand Metrics](https://substack.com/redirect/b7882f71-23dc-425d-b29e-b92f9c2d81a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) says its SDK-free interactive measurement solution signals a new era of interactive CTV advertising.
* New ad policy for [Beaumont city transit](https://substack.com/redirect/2fc6768b-aceb-4d7c-9622-403af96f291f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will fund improvements.
* DAX brings [DISQO’s measurement tools](https://substack.com/redirect/5542e209-e579-46bc-89d9-df2589f23926?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to every audio campaign.
* Index Exchange launches Gracenote-powered show-level reporting with [Spectrum Reach](https://substack.com/redirect/34e1859c-c94d-4770-aa11-5cbae92ef772?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* DoubleVerify launches [new solution](https://substack.com/redirect/970b6114-a73d-4449-8991-540fd07abd4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to improve brand alignment, performance for CTV.
* TripleLift announces partnership with [Attain](https://substack.com/redirect/2a82cbb9-8fec-4b00-b0da-4ff5cc790c86?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to unify context and commerce.
* PHȲND and Magnite open [smart TV gaming](https://substack.com/redirect/3ed3109a-d448-456f-a2b6-c31cbebed049?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to programmatic advertisers.

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">The first full week of 2026 was pretty full and kind of consuming.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f5b89801-d7c6-48f3-a6d1-ba71166602d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5f68fa55-f8d6-43b3-834f-d65907ed7539?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Reddit</a><span> launches automated ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a613d868-6594-48a6-b498-76c33cb978e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">max campaigns</a><span>’ to simplify ad buying and surface deeper audience insights.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Pinterest’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1ca1ecc3-3b37-400c-be26-44b43ed3a769?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new shoppable CTV series</a><span> turns pins Into purchases on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e730941-c302-4b7f-8103-1d1ac7708cba?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Roku</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fe66e4c9-ceb5-495c-bc3a-971f629ba6b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Discord</a><span> is said to file </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f92150a9-8f22-4013-9af4-734267c45002?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">confidentially</a><span> for IPO.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI unveils </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3fee5d0f-064e-470e-b3cc-d116c0dffc77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ChatGPT Health</a><span>, says 230 million users </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a1b1f9be-b01f-4719-ada6-9da1f98df868?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ask about health</a><span> each week.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>No, Grok </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/33c78443-a6b9-4f81-87a9-c72605a9f77f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">hasn’t paywalled</a><span> its deepfake image feature.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Senators </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/686c60b6-97d5-459a-b610-f610a5784781?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">urged</a><span> Apple and Google to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f7b036fc-08c8-404d-abd2-631d45e455e7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">remove X and Grok</a><span> from app stores over sexual deepfakes.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google: Don’t make “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d7e5b8dc-2b36-473f-9ecb-a0a5e9f43a4a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bite-sized</a><span>” content for LLMs if you care about search rank.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f891944-ce90-4a92-b4f3-679552fcfbbe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2df4de65-7b9d-4071-a265-6afb5b8a4c6b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Debunking</a><span> the AI food delivery hoax that fooled Reddit.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1c3bbb68-ca4e-404e-818c-03e81a69d212?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reportedly</a><span> getting ready to test ads in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eda2430e-24b9-4363-b256-81cd1eed02e7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ChatGPT</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube’s unskippable ads are out of control, but </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a5fa7d8-ed65-4c7b-bcdf-5f4eff248e68?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">one country</a><span> just stepped in to push back.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Spotify no longer running ICE recruitment ads, after </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a27d67b7-f9f8-46ad-afa9-36a23407c01a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">US government campaign</a><span> ends.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aae1c231-815e-41d1-a463-5f1fa61fe67c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">influencer culture</a><span> ruined a once-pristine national park lake.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>US appeals court appears skeptical of Meta, social media companies’ bid to cut off </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/38acfe78-27c8-49fa-a89b-396fff0523ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">addiction lawsuits</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Social media ban for under-15s: What we know about the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/04f3a0ef-c2ae-4ea9-a8c3-ca6ba5c639f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">French government’s plan</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amazon’s AI assistant </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/744eb6ab-04d4-4bda-82bb-c89b853f9700?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">comes to the web</a><span> with Alexa.com.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2cc93c2-0b4d-4a25-ba28-c2ef4e4a26ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Smart Rings</a><span> poised to go mainstream after a blockbuster 2025.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e8d865a4-0a9f-43a3-bb3c-fb7a104ce5ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Nvidia</a><span> hires Google Veteran as its first Chief Marketing Officer.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Ban on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/404e88c6-6a71-485c-a096-4ee85a8f1e02?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TV junk food advertising</a><span> before 9pm comes into force in UK.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3f8b8db4-341e-4dab-a050-e02330405a03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>The Bimma x Tracksuit: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">2025 Collab of the Year Report</a><span> is finally here…</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>For the first time, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9b1a918f-45aa-4c34-b46b-9d1c2a610168?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bimma Williams</a><span> partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">analyzed</a><span> the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>And built the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span>: an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span> blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked and why:</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists</p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b74166d5-68e6-4a97-a28b-f8b9348db846?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Collab of the Year</a><span> report.</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/40b72fc1-7475-4224-8d14-07cb2796bfaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Microsoft</a><span> launches Copilot Checkout and Brand Agents.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2813b1b3-40fe-4bd1-8971-3ebb3285d8a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">announces</a><span> AI Mode Checkout Protocol, Business Agent.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Walmart </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b2f6622b-e63a-4d72-a82c-848a8a534313?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">opens up ads</a><span> in Gen AI Shopping Agent Sparky.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google, Shopify and retailers push </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b9a3cd55-bbd1-4ba0-a40b-2ae90c5fd7f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI shopping</a><span> standard.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Amazon’s AI shopping tool </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/67a212e5-e7a4-4bc9-872f-7a70b7606ca7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sparks backlash </a><span>from online retailers that didn’t want websites scraped.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>NYC Wegmans is</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ab4e484f-7a33-461e-8933-7d2b7e1371e2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> storing biometric data</a><span> on shoppers’ eyes, voices and faces.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>NBCU says </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aa1fa41a-bb5a-4c7f-8b60-a0bef2c70f2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">2026 Winter Olympics ads</a><span> have sold out.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Disney nearly sold out of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e5649ffe-f8c0-48b7-8baa-24fd6010a78f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad inventory</a><span> for college football championship.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4d6e3548-b967-44ba-8123-177d2ee8476b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Beehiiv</a><span> doubles ad sales team, scaling newsletter ads business.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Roblox </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9d0ad9e3-bb8e-47fd-85cf-327bdf8dc68a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expands</a><span> advertising platform as </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2dcbc55e-2393-4c9e-b685-7da80989eb81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">essential channel</a><span> for the next generations.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brands spent </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/05944813-9fd9-431a-b09a-797cca549781?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more than $1 billion</a><span> on celebrities in ads last year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c56b0787-aa86-4987-bada-a7cb68fdba17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">PayPal Ads</a><span> launches transaction graph insights and measurement </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cf586585-bd69-4432-bc77-de24997d28b2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to improve </a><span>commerce advertising outcomes.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Marc Lore’s Wonder aims to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a377c1c5-18d6-4cea-8e94-b66f83626621?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">transform restaurants</a><span> with automation.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Retailers like Kroger, CVS plan many </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/16b36ffb-b61d-463d-aba6-3b547b823666?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more screens for in-store ads</a><span> in 2026.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Meet the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4f6f42a0-5a99-489c-9d5a-93e5b13da058?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new tech laws</a><span> of 2026.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Conservative lawmakers want </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad020b04-0c4e-4d6b-a1c0-fb047e57e18f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">porn taxes</a><span>. Critics say they’re unconstitutional.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Italy fines Cloudflare </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d30677a8-a4f8-442f-8900-132142266e71?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">14M euros</a><span> for not blocking pirate sites on 1.1.1.1 DNS service.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bbd4bdac-07c2-4b49-abee-c283963e6a54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/c0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":61458,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!dE8f!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>I usually don’t care for whatever Adam Mosseri has to say about the state of Instagram, but I guess a lot of people do, and they are writing about the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/14a807e0-e30f-4b56-8aa6-c1e48a2ce15c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> recent essay and statements</a><span> made by the Instagram CEO regarding the rise of AI content and the future of Instagram.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">First, I sincerely think people lack context. Go through my newsletters from 2025. Meta has made a gazillion statements about AI content being the future, and then compare a decade of statements made by Zuckerberg and Mosseri. They are both on different wavelengths and always make opposite statements to create a balance. Second, half of Instagram is ads. Meta has already integrated Gen AI creative production into their advertising platform, and it’s only going to get worse.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Based on just these two points, you should be questioning yourself. Why should I trust Mosseri? Now, let’s say we trust Mosseri and the arguments still don’t hold merit.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">He says, “The key risk that a platform like Instagram faces is that, as the world inevitably changes more and more quickly, the platform fails to keep up.” Most social media trends still take a couple of days to reach Instagram. What are we talking about?</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Then he says, “Creators matter more. Power has been shifting from institutions to individuals for years because the internet made it so anyone with a compelling idea could find an audience.” Where is the monetization program you promised a decade ago?</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">He highlighted this part to make a big point. “The bar is going to shift from ‘can you create?’ to ‘can you make something that only you could create?’” Again, your company is creating fake SEO titles for Google rankings, AI-generated search tags on top of comments, and anything remotely new gets pushed aside because the algorithm cannot compare it to other content. </p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="656"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/023cc0de-b59d-45b9-8437-fe804f1f6bfd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/8454bae1-f724-43b2-9a6c-6c12d2b480c5\_1258x431.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":431,"width":1258,"resizeWidth":656,"bytes":87072,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/184158130?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F8454bae1-f724-43b2-9a6c-6c12d2b480c5\_1258x431.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="188.43402225755167" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!Wzd3!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F8454bae1-f724-43b2-9a6c-6c12d2b480c5\_1258x431.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Last, he shifted the blame to camera companies, saying, “Just as AI makes polish cheap, phone cameras have made professional looking imagery ubiquitous. Both trends cheapen the aesthetic.” Dude, in 2016 to 2018, and even today, some people call out influencers using the term “Instagram face” because your platform gave rise to all types of filters and beauty touches and played a huge role in teens feeling bad about themselves. Your company tried to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/849e9080-eb69-4ebc-a422-3d8169ad0e3a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bury research</a><span> exposing the negative impact this content had on teenage girls.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">“That feed is dead. People largely stopped sharing personal moments to feed years ago.” Again, I wonder who caused that. It happened before TikTok. You removed the chronological feed and sent teens looking for other content because your algorithm was too busy showing kids perfect influencers. And yeah, then your app also showed them soft porn. It did that again last year, while also showing drug ads.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Near the end of the essay, he says, “It will be more practical to fingerprint real media than fake media,” and it’s true. I agree with that, because it will be unprofitable to exclude free AI content that they themselves can create.</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Mosseri says, “In a world of infinite abundance and infinite doubt, the creators who can maintain trust and signal authenticity by being real, transparent, and consistent will stand out.”</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Almost a million CEOs have uttered a version of this statement on stages. </p>
</blockquote>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Guess who else made authenticity and original content a big deal? It was </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2dfdf0f0-b4d9-4008-855b-fa2f6d5f8500?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google</a><span> in 2024. And where is Google in 2026? AI overviews everywhere. </span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">TL;DR: The master manipulator rebranded Instagram’s flaws as a cultural failure and used arguments you often see in every other write up about the decline of social media.</p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b62c8512-0967-4504-ae5e-c52ece6dd78a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">password reset</a><span> attacks — What you need to check right now.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>More bad news for Linktree-like platforms as Instagram tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/95c40f05-a7d2-4062-b854-49711500f4ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a shareable link</a><span> that opens a user’s profile links section.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is now working on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e15e69d-462b-41c6-9816-d0d649d367ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI voice filters</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Edits App tests ability </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc8ad5ae-ab12-4db7-9197-9f8b3fa6ae1d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to link</a><span> Instagram account.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads has begun displaying “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/875b1324-4a28-4d68-ad7b-f6232b8d4851?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ghost Posts</a><span>” on profiles.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>IG is working on the ability to log in via </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/80bdd6af-aa9c-43a5-b588-9c1c006f0827?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">QR code</a><span> on tablets.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Threads is working on a new feature for Communities: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c692d7e8-51a4-4d32-84af-8174801dd4e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Live Chat</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The platform is also working on a new attachment option for posts: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99e2d33b-203e-4962-998a-1923659809c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tangle</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads makes another attempt to</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f8343a9-c82c-48cc-9949-686a33348899?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> lure sports Twitter users</a><span> with a basketball game for chats.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is working on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/35a6aa28-f54e-426a-96ae-ff4efafdb0e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI transitions</a><span> for photos in Stories.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;">Some other articles responding to the Instagram CEO’s statement:</p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Who decides what’s real in the age of AI? </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4a0252dd-822c-4c01-b0ca-5a1057190798?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> does.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is betting on the momentum, not the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/76f382b4-73d7-4d29-8754-b37d8e3d9eca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reaction</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/546dca00-cfc2-4241-8141-ad24dcdf92f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">repositions</a><span> competitive insights as another perk of Meta Verified.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🦀 Report Break: Capping off the holiday season, shoppers spent $257.8 billion online in the U.S. from Nov. 1 to Dec. 31, marking a 6.8% year-over-year increase, according to Adobe Analytics data </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9ed71d9f-63ba-489d-9198-c54d9d333af0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">released</a><span> this week. The company said spending beat its </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3d277943-b454-40d5-86c6-956b5b579bb7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prior forecast</a><span> of $253.4 billion in online sales for the season.</span></p></blockquote>

</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta strikes deals with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/996c5aad-00ba-4791-ac63-b25579183136?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">nuclear start-ups</a><span> to meet AI power demand</span></p></li>

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>China </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/df14370b-4a3f-4c99-b463-34d54af4c053?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to probe</a><span> Meta’s acquisition of AI outfit Manus.</span></p>

</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Countries are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2949c11-d3f1-485b-a25c-85daa46dca1f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">outlawing online gambling ads</a><span>. Meta is selling them anyway.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s Reality Labs chief is calling the ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1168490f-c317-434c-af1f-fbd3c0f1fa5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">most important</a><span>’ meeting of the year, urging employees to show up in person</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta created ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8820495b-fa73-4649-9a36-a7268e37c344?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">playbook</a><span>’ to fend off pressure to crack down on scammers, documents show.</span></p></li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google launches the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5cbddef5-9951-4915-8486-8f3c90452b67?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Universal Commerce Protocol (UCP)</a><span>, a new open standard for agentic commerce.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b1ea92a7-a6fe-4966-843b-5f1fdda8b626?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expanded video limits</a><span> in Performance Max.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/37380677-13d9-43c5-9f39-944ff176a680?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Core Before Christmas</a><span>’ – Google’s December 2025 Broad Core Update.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google quietly expands </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b5751cd3-b972-46ef-a01c-d020e502baab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">asset A/B testing</a><span> to all Performance Max campaigns.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5aeb4283-5e91-44b0-a69b-49fe48ba2d31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">recommender system</a><span> breakthrough detects semantic intent.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google AI Overviews are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/17782c41-3a56-4e27-bccd-d2d02e0670ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">tested and removed </a><span>based on engagement.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gemini will let you </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/26da826d-f2c1-4e38-87a9-1b02bd61846c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">control Google TV settings</a><span>, getting richer Photos support &amp; responses.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google rolls out</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0bde6ca5-3846-4019-a580-2099e07873f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> Tag Gateway integration</a><span> via Google Cloud.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1bcb8155-b454-4631-9820-23973abb9231?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google Classroom’s new tool </a><span>uses Gemini to transform lessons into podcast episodes.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google to allow </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/64c8f5c3-a433-40bd-8ae6-9019edc7268f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Prediction Markets ads</a><span> under strict rules.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>👀 Report Break: The “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/481065d3-b2f7-4e3b-adc8-f82749b300b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unexpected</a><span>” growth of 3.88% saw display advertising return to being the largest revenue category in the Digital Publishers’ Revenue Index in the third quarter of 2025 after being overtaken by subscriptions in Q1 2025 (and evenly matched in Q2).</span></p></blockquote>
